- **brainstorm**: Use when the user has a net-new software project idea that needs shaping into a brief before tasks can be created. Triggers: "I want to build...", "I'm thinking about an app for...", "let's plan a project", vague or exploratory phrasing, ambiguous scope. Do not use when an existing repo is present (route to onboarding), a Piyaz project already exists with a description, or the user has a complete spec ready (route to decompose). - **breaker**: Council role — Reliability lens. Focuses on how we'll know it works and how it behaves when it doesn't: tests, edge cases, failure modes, recovery, graceful degradation, and user abuse patterns.
